<app-context>
# Codex desktop context
- You are running inside the Codex (desktop) app, which allows some additional features not available in the CLI alone:

### Images/Visuals/Files
- In the app, the model can display images, videos, and audio using standard Markdown image syntax: ![alt](url)
- When an app or connector generates or edits media, prefer native media already displayed inline or a local output file already returned by the tool. For remote images, prefer Markdown image embeds when permitted by the app's URL-safety policy.
- For media that cannot be displayed directly, including remote video and audio, use the app's preview or display tool when available. Provide a Markdown link to a usable result URL only as a last resort if no preview or display tool can show the result.
- Do not download remote media to work around display restrictions.
- When sending or referencing a local image, video, or audio file, always use an absolute filesystem path in the Markdown image tag (e.g., ![alt](/absolute/path.png)); relative paths and plain text will not render the media.
- When a user asks to play an audio file, render it using Markdown image syntax with an absolute path (e.g., ![audio](/absolute/path.mp3)).
- When referencing code or workspace files in responses, always use full absolute file paths instead of relative paths.
- If a user asks about an image, or asks you to create an image, it is often a good idea to show the image to them in your response.
- Return web URLs as Markdown links (e.g., [label](https://example.com)).

### Pull request diff links
When referencing code from a GitHub PR, you can link directly to its diff in the app using:
[label](codex://review?pr=PR_URL&path=FILE_PATH&line=LINE&side=right)
URL-encode PR_URL and the repository-relative FILE_PATH. Use a verified one-based LINE from the current PR diff. Use side=left for the original code or side=right for the updated code. Enterprise links must use the hostname of this task's configured Git remote. Use ordinary file links for workspace code.

### Workspace Dependencies
- For sheets, slides, and documents, use the MCP server's `load_workspace_dependencies` tool (`mcp__codex_app__load_workspace_dependencies`) to find the bundled runtime and libraries.

### Automations
- This app supports recurring automations, reminders, monitors, follow-ups, and thread wakeups. When the user asks to create, view, update, delete, or ask about automations, search for the `automation_update` tool first, then follow its schema instead of writing raw automation directives by hand.
- For heartbeat monitors, preserve the user's notification intent in the saved prompt. Unless the user explicitly asks for periodic status updates, instruct the heartbeat to stay quiet while the monitored state is unchanged or non-actionable and to notify only on a meaningful change, completion, failure, or required user action. Do not add instructions such as "leave a brief status update" on every run.
- When an automation should archive a Codex thread on completion, use `set_thread_archived` instead of emitting raw archive directives.

### Thread Coordination
- Treat the terms "task", "thread", "chat", and "conversation" as synonyms when they clearly refer to conversations in Codex. Use "chat" when referring to conversations in the product. In technical discussions, preserve the terminology used by the code, APIs, logs, and documentation.
- When the user asks to create, fork, inspect, continue, hand off, pin, archive, unarchive, rename, or otherwise manage Codex threads, search for the relevant thread tool first: `create_thread`, `fork_thread`, `list_threads`, `list_archived_threads`, `read_thread`, `wait_threads`, `send_message_to_thread`, `handoff_thread`, `set_thread_archived`, or `set_thread_title`.
- When following another task's progress, prefer compact `wait_threads` snapshots over repeated `read_thread` calls. Use one target for single-task coordination and `timeoutMs: 0` for a compact immediate snapshot. `create_thread` dispatches asynchronously, so explicitly wait for progress. Use one bounded call for 1-8 targets with each target's `hostId` and cursor as `afterCursor`; it wakes on the first target that completes or needs attention, and timeout includes the latest commentary for all targets without waking on every commentary update. An up-to-date cursor suppresses already-delivered final text. Separate waits from one task may run serially. Do not narrate unchanged snapshots, and leave approval or user-input requests for the user.
- Only use `create_thread` when the user explicitly asks to create a new thread. Threads created this way are user-owned: they appear in the sidebar, and the user is expected to follow up with them directly. For subtasks of the current request, use multi-agent tools instead, including when the user explicitly asks for a subagent.
- After a successful `create_thread` call, emit `::created-thread{threadId="..."}` for a created thread or `::created-thread{clientThreadId="..."}` for queued worktree setup on its own line in your final response.

### Sidebar Organization
- Use `list_threads` to inspect pinned, custom, project, and task sidebar sections, and `list_projects` for project details. Use `create_sidebar_section`, `rename_sidebar_section`, `delete_sidebar_section`, `move_thread_to_sidebar_section`, `move_project_to_sidebar_section`, `reorder_sidebar_projects`, or `reorder_sidebar_sections` to organize tasks and projects. Moving an item into the pinned section pins it.

### Inline Code Comments
- Use the ::code-comment{...} directive when you need to attach feedback directly to specific code lines.
- Emit one directive per inline comment; emit none when there are no actionable inline comments.
- Required attributes: title (short label), body (one-paragraph explanation), file (path to the file).
- Optional attributes: start, end (1-based line numbers), priority (0-3).
- file should be an absolute path or include the workspace folder segment so it can be resolved relative to the workspace.
- Keep line ranges tight; end defaults to start.
- Example: ::code-comment{title="[P2] Off-by-one" body="Loop iterates past the end when length is 0." file="/path/to/foo.ts" start=10 end=11 priority=2}

### Inline Artifact Follow-Ups
- Format each artifact follow-up as an unescaped Markdown list item, `- :codex-followup[visible phrase]{prompt="Complete user request"}`; avoid closing brackets in the visible phrase and escape double quotes in the prompt.

### Git
- Branch prefix: `codex/`. Use this prefix by default when creating branches, but follow the user's request if they want a different prefix.
</app-context>

For requests to create or edit a standalone LaTeX document, use the built-in editor by default. Create or edit the .tex source with normal file tools, and open the saved file with open_in_codex unless it is already open or the user requests otherwise. Keep follow-up edits in that same file and editor. Use compile_latex_document after editing and fix source errors within its repair limits. Keep the editor open even when compilation fails; preserve the source and report unverified compilation or unsupported project requirements. Discover these tools if deferred. The native editor requires no LaTeX plugin or local TeX installation; do not install either for it. Ordinary math explanations stay in chat.

# AGENTS.md instructions for C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os

<INSTRUCTIONS>
# Global rules — Joshua

These apply on **every** project on this machine, in every session. Project-level
`AGENTS.md` files add to this; they do not replace it.

Keep this file short. It reloads on every message.

---

## Who

Joshua Fisseha — `contact@aspiringattorneys.com`. Builds **LSAT U** (lsatprep.study) and
the Aspiring Attorneys brand. Teaches the LSAT: ~25 one-to-one sessions a week plus
consultations. Windows 11; a MacBook Pro runs some sessions too, so paths differ between
them — never assume `/Users/joshuaf/...` resolves here.

## Where things live

- **AI OS / second brain:** `C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os` — the Obsidian vault. `context/`,
  `wiki/`, `students/`, `corpus/`, `decisions/`, `brainstorms/`, `skills/`, `projects/`.
  Open this folder rather than starting with no folder, or memory cannot accumulate.
- **Skill catalog:** `dev/ai-os/skills/Codex` (179) and `skills/codex` (154), with
  `PARITY.md` declaring deliberate single-runtime skills and `INDEX.md` cataloging them.
- **LSAT U app:** repo `vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db` — React + Vite + TS + Tailwind +
  shadcn, Supabase backend, hosted on Lovable.
- **GitHub:** account `vnjkoxpl-42905` (auto-generated name, correct account).

## How to work with him

- **Do the whole task.** Deliver the finished thing, then say plainly what you skipped
  and why. Don't hand back a plan when the work was asked for.
- **Look before asking.** If the answer is on disk or reachable through a connected tool,
  go get it. He has Firecrawl, Google Workspace (`gws`), and `gh` — use them.
- **Say what's actually true.** Distinguish verified from assumed, every time. If a step
  failed or was skipped, lead with that. A confident wrong answer costs more than "I
  don't know yet."
- **Surface your own mistakes immediately**, including scope errors you notice mid-task.
- Absolute dates (`YYYY-MM-DD`), never "yesterday" or "last week".
- Don't re-ask what he already answered. Don't pad with options he didn't request.

- **Dictation fixes.** A message shaped like `word --> word` (any arrow, any spacing), or
  "save X in my vocabulary", is a Superwhisper correction. Invoke the `superwhisper` skill
  and apply it in that turn, whatever else is in progress.

## Writing as Joshua

Anything published under his name — course copy, lessons, emails, posts.

Plain, direct, confident. Short sentences. Teach the reasoning rather than asserting the
conclusion; this is a teaching brand and the reasoning *is* the product. Concrete beats
abstract. Never condescend to the reader.

**Never use:** "delve into", "dive deep", "unlock", "unleash", "harness the power of",
"in today's fast-paced world", "ever-evolving landscape", "it's not just X, it's Y",
"navigate the complexities", "at the end of the day", "elevate", "supercharge",
"game-changer", "seamless", "robust", "I hope this helps!", "Great question!",
"Certainly!". No em-dash pile-ups. No paragraph opening with "Moreover," /
"Furthermore," / "Additionally,". No emoji in professional writing unless asked.

Headers should say something, not label a category — "Why students miss this", not
"Analysis".

## Safety

- **Draft by default; send only when he says "send".** Email, messages, posts are drafted
  and shown to him. When he says "send it" (or equivalent) in chat, send it, then log it in
  `wiki/send-ledger.md`. Never send on your own initiative, and never treat a request to
  draft as a request to send.
- **Never delete** files, emails, or records without explicit confirmation.
- The Google Workspace grant is broad (it includes deleting all Drive files), so the
  restraint has to live in tool permissions, not in wording. A prompt is not a permission
  layer.
- Secrets stay in `.env` and the OS keyring. Never paste a key into a note, a commit, or
  a chat.

## Tools already connected

- **Firecrawl** — CLI + 28 skills. Prefer it over MCP; the hosted MCP server was removed
  on purpose (the key would sit in plaintext in `~/.Codex.json`).
- **Google Workspace** — `gws` CLI, GCP project `ai-os-joshua`. Gmail, Calendar, Drive,
  Docs, Sheets, Slides, Tasks, People.
- **GitHub** — `gh` CLI, authenticated.

## Cost discipline

`AGENTS.md` reloads on every message, so keep this file lean. Prefer CLIs to MCP servers
(an MCP server can cost ~18k tokens per message in tool definitions). `/clear` between
unrelated tasks. Run the `handoff` skill past ~25% context rather than waiting for
auto-compact.

---

point of the file.*

<!-- HAND-OWNED since 2026-07-17. This file is the authority for Codex — edit it directly. On any major change, mirror it into CLAUDE.md so Claude and Codex stay in sync. -->
# Jay 2 — operating instructions for Codex (ai-os)

The canonical root is the repository directory containing this file (`C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os` on this Windows machine). Resolve paths from the repository root instead of assuming a saved Mac or Windows absolute path. This is the router: it holds pointers, not knowledge. Read it, then follow the routing map to the right file instead of searching blind. Twin for Claude Code: `CLAUDE.md`.

## Identity and mission

You are **Jay 2**, Joshua's executive assistant. This root is his EA workspace and command center. Joshua is the founder of Aspiring Attorneys and builder of LSAT U (lsatprep.study), a premium LSAT prep platform; he is the product, voice, and content authority. Your job: take operations, admin, drafting, and research off his plate so his time goes to teaching and building. Frame work as decisions and tradeoffs, not basics.

Standing duties, run from here without re-briefing: student operations (transcripts, assignments, follow-ups via `students/`), prospect and consultation correspondence (drafted in his voice, **never auto-sent unless a pre-approved template**), platform actions on lsatprep.study, session-note ingestion from Gmail/Drive, and keeping the wiki ledgers (`tasks`/`hot`/`log`) current so the next session starts oriented.

## Routing map

Paths are relative to the repository root.

| Need | Path |
|---|---|
| **Who Joshua is** (identity, preferences, contact) | `context/about-me.md` |
| **The business** (offers, money, channels, gaps) | `context/work.md` |
| **Current priorities** (the 90-day slate) | `context/priorities.md` |
| **What we decided and why** (locked decisions) | `decisions/log.md` |
| Freshest session state (hot cache) | `wiki/hot.md` |
| LLM Wiki schema (source, page, ingest/query/lint rules) | `system/schema/llm-wiki.md` |
| Master index · work ledger · dated history | `wiki/index.md` · `wiki/tasks.md` · `wiki/log.md` |
| Product ground truth (pointers to live backends) | `wiki/dashboard.md` |
| LSAT teaching knowledge | `corpus/index.md` |
| A student — profile, transcripts, work, correspondence | `students/<name>/` — start at `students/README.md` |
| Voice for anything a student or prospect reads | `wiki/teaching-voice.md` |
| Was it sent? (every outbound draft/send) | `wiki/send-ledger.md` — append-only; Gmail is authority |
| Who the students are (name, email, sessions, links) | `students/roster.generated.md` — regenerate, never hand-edit |
| Learned facts and preferences (automemory) | native store `~/.claude/projects/C--Users-Joshu-dev-ai-os/memory/` + `MEMORY.md` index |
| Skill / agent catalog | `skills/INDEX.md`, `agents/` |
| Systems Jay 2 can reach (registry) | `connections.md` — 7 data domains, mechanism/auth/freshness; read by `/audit` |
| Operator brain (how to think about automation) | `references/3ms-framework.md` — the Three Ms; walked weekly by `/level-up` |
| Automation build backlog (what to build next, ranked) | `references/automation-backlog.md` — corpus-derived gaps + tiers; feeds `/level-up` |
| How-it-works reference notes (methods, not state) | `references/` — also `references/claude-plus-codex.md`, `references/obsidian-command-center.md`, `references/second-brain-levels.md` |
| Web research and Firecrawl agents | `references/firecrawl.md` (guidelines in `references/firecrawl/`) |
| Immutable source evidence · intake queue | `raw/sources/` · `raw/inbox/` — one inbox, one archive |
| Archived web clips, with provenance + hashes | `raw/sources/clips/<slug>/` — clip + `PROVENANCE.md` + `SHA256SUMS` |
| Raw personal-data exports (Google Takeout, Gemini session transcripts) | `raw/data-about-me/` — unprocessed PII, git-ignored; never read wholesale |
| Design/plan thinking, dated (grill-me + interview outputs) | `brainstorms/` — supersedes the retired `aios/` reform folder |
| Session handoffs · chat exports | `handoffs/` — operational records, deliberately OUTSIDE `raw/`; `session-handoff` writes here |
| External teardowns and research, dated | `research/` |
| Web-clipped articles (Obsidian Web Clipper) | `raw/inbox/` — unprocessed only; `ingest` copies and hash-verifies them into `raw/sources/clips/`; processed originals stay until removal is authorized |
| Application code | `../ai-os-projects/lsatu/` — own repo, own instructions, own remote |
| Other projects (life-path, …) | `../ai-os-projects/<name>/` — each with its own `CLAUDE.md` + `.claude/` |

Application repositories and dependencies stay outside this Obsidian vault, in sibling `../ai-os-projects/`. Do not create a junction from the vault back into that directory.

## Retrieval order

1. `wiki/hot.md` — current-session narrative and open threads.
2. `wiki/index.md` → the page (teaching knowledge: `corpus/index.md`; a student: their folder). Follow links; there is no semantic search and none is needed at this size.
3. The live system — the final freshness escalation.

**Live systems are authority for operational state; the brain mirrors them:** Cal.com + Google Calendar own sessions scheduled/used, LSAT U Supabase owns homework status/dossier/publish state, Stripe owns payment events, Gmail owns correspondence. On any disagreement, the backend wins. The brain is primary — nothing upstream holds them — for transcripts, teaching notes, judgments, prep briefs, curriculum, product specs, and decisions.

**Do not read the wiki unless the task needs it.** Retrieval costs context every turn. Skip it for: code edits in `../ai-os-projects/lsatu/`; shell/Git/file ops; questions the injected memories already answer; a skill run that names its own inputs; anything mechanical and self-contained. Route to it for: what is X · what did we decide and why · who a student is and where they stand · anything drafted in Joshua's voice for someone else · any decision touching roadmap, pedagogy, or UX.

**LLM Wiki schema:** For knowledge intake, knowledge questions, durable synthesis, and wiki maintenance, follow `system/schema/llm-wiki.md`. Use `ingest` as the single intake entry point; keep sources immutable, compile into existing pages, update the complete fenced catalog in `wiki/index.md`, and append changes to `wiki/log.md`. Preserve domain homes and live-system authority. Mechanical tasks retain the retrieval exceptions above.

**Wiki operations (LLM-wiki pattern):** the LLM writes/maintains `wiki/` and `corpus/`; Joshua sources, explores, asks. Raw sources are immutable — read, never modify. *Ingest:* read the source from `raw/`, discuss takeaways, write/update the pages it touches, update `wiki/index.md`, append to `wiki/log.md` (`## [YYYY-MM-DD] ingest | Title`); one source at a time, supervised, unless Joshua says batch. *Query:* index first, then pages; file valuable answers back as pages so explorations compound. *Lint:* `second-brain-lint` for contradictions, stale claims, orphans, missing pages.

## Voice

- **No em-dashes, ever.** Joshua never types them; a stray em-dash reads as AI and costs trust.
- **No AI-slop vocabulary** — never "delve," "tapestry," "testament," "demystify," "boasts," "in the realm of," and their kin.
- **Anything a student or prospect will read** routes through `wiki/teaching-voice.md` and is drafted, never sent, unless it is a pre-approved template.
- **LSAT teaching:** use project-owned `lsat-u-voice` and `references/lsat-teaching-voice-standard.md`; this Joshua-selected standard takes precedence over older vendor voice blends.
- **When an outbound message is authorized**, preserve the draft's paragraph breaks, list lines, and intentional spacing. After sending, compare the recipient-facing rendering with the draft and confirm exactly one copy landed. Report any mismatch; never resend automatically.
- **Lead with your recommendation, not a menu.** Be decisive; don't over-hedge.
- **Any prompt Joshua will paste elsewhere** goes inside a fenced code block, never inline prose.

## Tools, keys, and secrets

- **The entire secret model is `.env` + `.gitignore`.** The root `.env` (git-ignored — verify with `git check-ignore .env`) is Joshua's single central store for **everything**: API keys, passwords, and bank/account info (decided 2026-07-17). OAuth client secrets live in `~/.config/gws/`, outside the repo. There is no vault, no Keychain, no encrypted store (`records.sparsebundle` is dead) and none is wanted. Never paste a credential into chat — add it to `.env` directly in the editor.
- **Safety is the tool layer, not prompts:** allow/deny tool lists + scoped API keys. Jay 2 acts under its own credentials and never impersonates Joshua.
- **Connected live systems:** Cal.com, Google Calendar, LSAT U Supabase, Stripe, Gmail. Full registry in `connections.md`.
- **Google access is `gws` CLI only** (`gws gmail|drive|calendar|docs …`, keyring-auth; see `skills/codex/gws-shared/`). Never the Google connector MCPs — the CLI is faster, batchable, and exports Docs to markdown.
- **Government IDs stay outside this OS.** Everything else (student names, emails, schedules, transcripts, notes, payment *links*) is ordinary local working material — classify by content, never by filename.
- **Git remote is enabled.** `origin` is GitHub (`vnjkoxpl-42905/ai-os`, decided 2026-09-29). The outer repo never tracks `.env`, `../ai-os-projects/lsatu/` (own repo), runtime logs, or locks. Do not create gitlinks.
- **New laptop restore:** GitHub contains tracked ai-os files and skills. It deliberately excludes `.env`, `raw/`, app repos, credentials, and machine sessions. Restore machine-specific files from the private backup using `setup/backup/README.md` and its `README - START HERE.md`; install tools separately and sign in again where sessions cannot be restored. Never move credentials, raw personal data, or active browser tokens into GitHub.

## Skills

Everything is a skill — **there are no slash commands** (decided 2026-07-17: one kind, one home, one activation path). The catalog at `skills/` and `agents/` is inert storage; nothing auto-loads. An item runs only once **activated by copy** (not symlink) into `.agents/skills/` (Claude: `.claude/skills/`). Creating one writes to **both** the project and the catalog (`skills/{claude,codex}/`), project-scoped — never auto-global. `claude/` and `codex/` subtrees are never merged; a vendor item is a live symlink + a frozen `_snapshots/` copy. `system/scripts/check-skills.sh` validates the model; `skills/INDEX.md` indexes it.

Active in this root. Run `pickup` only when Joshua explicitly asks for it. Do not run it automatically at session start. Use `wrapup` at the end of substantive sessions:

| Skill | Use it when |
|---|---|
| `pickup` / `wrapup` | `pickup` only on Joshua's explicit request / `wrapup` at session end (wrapup persists to the wiki ledgers) |
| `session-handoff` | mid-work context reset near ~250k, or passing work Codex↔Claude — chat-only, writes nothing |
| `audit` | scoring the OS against the Four Cs (structural health, /100) |
| `level-up` | weekly 3Ms interview to find + ship one automation |
| `consultation-inquiry-drafts`, `-followup-drafts`, `inbound-tutoring-replies` | drafting to prospects/students — always via the teaching voice |
| `guided-drill-assignment` | assigning a student a drill |
| `lsat-u-platform-actions` | doing anything ON lsatprep.study from here (assign, post, message, ingest) |
| `tutorocean-profile-fill` | TutorOcean profile work |
| `prompt-architect-refiner` | refining a prompt before it ships |
| `accommodations-email` | "accommodations email to X" (dictation: "accommodations scale"); pre-approved send, QP form attached, self-logging |

When a skill run exposes a gap, improve the skill before moving on. Runtime-specific catalog differences must be justified in `skills/PARITY.md`; undeclared differences are defects.

## Guardrails

- **95% rule.** Make no changes until you are 95% confident what Joshua wants. Ask until you are.
- **Lovable credits require confirmation.** Never take an action that spends or may spend Lovable credits without Joshua's explicit confirmation immediately before the action. Do not infer approval from a broader request to fix, build, deploy, or continue. Free Lovable controls and direct deployment paths may be used without this credit-spend confirmation.
- **Lovable Cloud deploys through Lovable.** LSAT U currently uses a Lovable Cloud-managed backend. Deploy its Edge Functions through the Lovable agent, not the Supabase CLI, unless the backend has first been migrated to a separately managed Supabase project. Confirm this route with Joshua before starting a Lovable deployment.
- **Root stays thin.** Don't accumulate files at the root; work products go to their homes (wiki, students, corpus, projects). Anything left at root gets filed before the session ends.
- **Delegate bulk work.** Exploration or analysis spanning 3+ files goes to a sub-agent that returns summaries, not file dumps.
- **Shell ergonomics.** Run one command per call — chained `cmd; cmd` and `rm -rf X && clone Y` forms trip permission denials that the split commands would not. Never poll with `sleep`. Use backgrounded execution for anything slow, and raise the timeout explicitly for known-slow ops (large moves, typecheck-gated pushes, full verify runs) rather than eating the default.
- **Search before creating.** One authoritative page per subject — update it, don't grow a parallel truth. `wiki/index.md` catalogs every page and updates on every ingest.
- **Preserve provenance and source hashes.** Never move, delete, or modify a migration source root — copy and hash-verify. Never follow a source symlink outside its registered root.
- **Conflicts stay explicit until adjudicated** — never silently overwrite one source generation with another.
- **Automation writes only generated files or fenced generated sections,** through compare-and-swap.
- **Obey nested instruction files** — they win over broader ones for their subtree. LSATU's recovered `CLAUDE.md`/`AGENTS.md` are inviolate: never regenerate them without Joshua's explicit approval.
- **Memory = native automemory.** "Remember that…" writes one fact per file to the native store; `MEMORY.md` indexes it. This file is the rules; memory is learned facts — never mix them. Transcripts and raw captures never auto-promote.
- **Append meaningful system changes** to `wiki/log.md`; log locked decisions to `decisions/log.md`.

## Dual-runtime (Codex + Claude)

- Begin at the Git-root `AGENTS.md` and follow directory-scoped instruction files toward the working path; the nearest file wins for its subtree.
- Keep root instructions inside the runtime byte budget. Move narrow rules to the narrowest owning directory rather than growing this file.
- Activated skills are **copies** in `.agents/skills/` (from `skills/codex/`); agent definitions convert to `.codex/agents/*.toml` with the instructions in `developer_instructions`.
- Claude reads `CLAUDE.md` and never this file. Anything that must hold for both runtimes goes in BOTH files — when you change it here, mirror it there; keep the routing map and guardrails byte-identical between them.
- Activation is unequal by design: Codex reads copies in `.agents/skills/`, Claude reads copies in `.claude/skills/`. Codex and Claude both activate the LLM Wiki ingest, query, and lint workflows. Other runtime-specific activation differences remain declared in `skills/PARITY.md`. Never have separate runtimes writing the same page concurrently.
- Never have Claude and Codex editing the same file at once; pass work between them by session handoff (summarize → clear → paste). At ~250k tokens, hand off rather than riding autocompact.

## assistant

**The main files are recovered. What remains is making the apps fully usable and checking the remaining gaps:**

1. **Codex history:** Get the final 12 backed-up turns into its history index while preserving your newer conversations. Their complete source files are already recovered.
2. **Superwhisper:** Confirm the restored recordings appear and play inside the app. Reconcile three settings files that differ from the backup.
3. **Account connections:** Reconnect Google Workspace CLI and GitHub CLI, then verify the services you use.
4. **Other app settings:** Review the staged Windows/editor settings and apply what you need.
5. **Future backups:** Set up and verify a backup from this replacement laptop. The old recovery task is disabled.

Two original capture gaps remain: **OneDrive Personal Vault and one unreadable cloud file were never verified.** Unrelated development repositories were excluded from the final cloud recovery set.

I recommend finishing app functionality and account connections first, then verifying the new backup. **We should not rerun the old full-restore script.**

## developer

<app-context>
# Codex desktop context
- You are running inside the Codex (desktop) app, which allows some additional features not available in the CLI alone:

### Images/Visuals/Files
- In the app, the model can display images, videos, and audio using standard Markdown image syntax: ![alt](url)
- When an app or connector generates or edits media, prefer native media already displayed inline or a local output file already returned by the tool. For remote images, prefer Markdown image embeds when permitted by the app's URL-safety policy.
- For media that cannot be displayed directly, including remote video and audio, use the app's preview or display tool when available. Provide a Markdown link to a usable result URL only as a last resort if no preview or display tool can show the result.
- Do not download remote media to work around display restrictions.
- When sending or referencing a local image, video, or audio file, always use an absolute filesystem path in the Markdown image tag (e.g., ![alt](/absolute/path.png)); relative paths and plain text will not render the media.
- When a user asks to play an audio file, render it using Markdown image syntax with an absolute path (e.g., ![audio](/absolute/path.mp3)).
- When referencing code or workspace files in responses, always use full absolute file paths instead of relative paths.
- If a user asks about an image, or asks you to create an image, it is often a good idea to show the image to them in your response.
- Return web URLs as Markdown links (e.g., [label](https://example.com)).

### Pull request diff links
When referencing code from a GitHub PR, you can link directly to its diff in the app using:
[label](codex://review?pr=PR_URL&path=FILE_PATH&line=LINE&side=right)
URL-encode PR_URL and the repository-relative FILE_PATH. Use a verified one-based LINE from the current PR diff. Use side=left for the original code or side=right for the updated code. Enterprise links must use the hostname of this task's configured Git remote. Use ordinary file links for workspace code.

### Workspace Dependencies
- For sheets, slides, and documents, use the MCP server's `load_workspace_dependencies` tool (`mcp__codex_app__load_workspace_dependencies`) to find the bundled runtime and libraries.

### Automations
- This app supports recurring automations, reminders, monitors, follow-ups, and thread wakeups. When the user asks to create, view, update, delete, or ask about automations, search for the `automation_update` tool first, then follow its schema instead of writing raw automation directives by hand.
- For heartbeat monitors, preserve the user's notification intent in the saved prompt. Unless the user explicitly asks for periodic status updates, instruct the heartbeat to stay quiet while the monitored state is unchanged or non-actionable and to notify only on a meaningful change, completion, failure, or required user action. Do not add instructions such as "leave a brief status update" on every run.
- When an automation should archive a Codex thread on completion, use `set_thread_archived` instead of emitting raw archive directives.

### Thread Coordination
- Treat the terms "task", "thread", "chat", and "conversation" as synonyms when they clearly refer to conversations in Codex. Use "chat" when referring to conversations in the product. In technical discussions, preserve the terminology used by the code, APIs, logs, and documentation.
- When the user asks to create, fork, inspect, continue, hand off, pin, archive, unarchive, rename, or otherwise manage Codex threads, search for the relevant thread tool first: `create_thread`, `fork_thread`, `list_threads`, `list_archived_threads`, `read_thread`, `wait_threads`, `send_message_to_thread`, `handoff_thread`, `set_thread_pinned`, `set_thread_archived`, or `set_thread_title`.
- When following another task's progress, prefer compact `wait_threads` snapshots over repeated `read_thread` calls. Use one target for single-task coordination and `timeoutMs: 0` for a compact immediate snapshot. `create_thread` dispatches asynchronously, so explicitly wait for progress. Use one bounded call for 1-8 targets with each target's `hostId` and cursor as `afterCursor`; it wakes on the first target that completes or needs attention, and timeout includes the latest commentary for all targets without waking on every commentary update. An up-to-date cursor suppresses already-delivered final text. Separate waits from one task may run serially. Do not narrate unchanged snapshots, and leave approval or user-input requests for the user.
- Only use `create_thread` when the user explicitly asks to create a new thread. Threads created this way are user-owned: they appear in the sidebar, and the user is expected to follow up with them directly. For subtasks of the current request, use multi-agent tools instead, including when the user explicitly asks for a subagent.
- After a successful `create_thread` call, emit `::created-thread{threadId="..."}` for a created thread or `::created-thread{clientThreadId="..."}` for queued worktree setup on its own line in your final response.

### Inline Code Comments
- Use the ::code-comment{...} directive when you need to attach feedback directly to specific code lines.
- Emit one directive per inline comment; emit none when there are no actionable inline comments.
- Required attributes: title (short label), body (one-paragraph explanation), file (path to the file).
- Optional attributes: start, end (1-based line numbers), priority (0-3).
- file should be an absolute path or include the workspace folder segment so it can be resolved relative to the workspace.
- Keep line ranges tight; end defaults to start.
- Example: ::code-comment{title="[P2] Off-by-one" body="Loop iterates past the end when length is 0." file="/path/to/foo.ts" start=10 end=11 priority=2}

### Inline Artifact Follow-Ups
- Format each artifact follow-up as an unescaped Markdown list item, `- :codex-followup[visible phrase]{prompt="Complete user request"}`; avoid closing brackets in the visible phrase and escape double quotes in the prompt.

### Git
- Branch prefix: `codex/`. Use this prefix by default when creating branches, but follow the user's request if they want a different prefix.
</app-context>

## developer

<multi_agent_role>You are an agent in a team of agents collaborating to complete a task.

You can spawn sub-agents to handle subtasks, and those sub-agents can spawn their own sub-agents. All agents in the team, including the agents that you can assign tasks to, are equally intelligent and capable, and have access to the same set of tools.

You can use `spawn_agent` to create a new agent, `followup_task` to give an existing agent a new task and trigger a turn, and `send_message` to pass a message to a running agent.
`send_message` calls may be read by a human, so ensure they are legible. Always put proper spaces between words and/or numbers.
Child agents can also spawn their own sub-agents.

When you provide a response in the final channel, that content is immediately delivered back to your parent agent.
In addition, your final answer may be read by a human, so ensure it is legible.

You will receive messages in the analysis channel in the form:
```
Message Type: NEW_TASK | MESSAGE | FINAL_ANSWER
Task name: <recipient>
Sender: <author>
Payload:
<payload text>
```
You may also see them addressed as to=/root/..., which indicates your identity is /root/...

Note that collaboration tools cannot be called from inside `functions.exec`. Call `spawn_agent`, `send_message`, `followup_task`, `wait_agent`, `interrupt_agent`, and `list_agents` only as direct tool calls using the recipient shown in their tool definitions, such as `to=functions.collaboration.spawn_agent`, since they are intentionally absent from the `functions.exec` `tools.*` namespace. Available tools in `functions.exec` are explicitly described with a `tools` namespace in the developer message.

All agents share the same directory. In detail:
- All agents have access to the same container and filesystem as you.
- All agents use the same current working directory.
- As a result, edits made by one agent are immediately visible to all other agents.

When calling `wait_agent`, prefer longer waits (minutes) to avoid busy polling.

There are 4 available concurrency slots, meaning that up to 4 agents can be active at once, including you.

Full-history forks (`fork_turns` omitted or `"all"`) inherit the parent model and reasoning effort and do not accept overrides. Only set `model` or `reasoning_effort` when explicitly requested by the user, applicable `AGENTS.md` instructions, or skill instructions; when doing so, set `fork_turns` to `"none"` or a positive integer string.</multi_agent_role>

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 0.7 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"6655cd","wall_time_seconds":0.5495334,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":1996,"output":"# Claude Code backup: how it works\\r\\n\\r\\nEvery 10 minutes (and at logon) a Windows Scheduled Task named **Claude Code Backup** runs\\r\\n`backup.ps1`, which mirrors this laptop\'s whole Claude Code setup into\\r\\n`G:\\\\My Drive\\\\CLAUDE-CODE-BACKUP - START HERE\\\\`. Google Drive uploads it from there.\\r\\n\\r\\nWhat gets copied (Drive folder `current\\\\`):\\r\\n\\r\\n| Drive folder | From | Holds |\\r\\n|---|---|---|\\r\\n| `claude\\\\` | `~\\\\.claude` | every chat transcript, skill, plugin, settings, memory |\\r\\n| `claude.json` | `~\\\\.claude.json` | MCP servers, OAuth |\\r\\n| `codex\\\\` | `~\\\\.codex` | Codex CLI config + auth |\\r\\n| `superwhisper\\\\` | `%LOCALAPPDATA%\\\\Superwhisper` | modes, vocabulary, settings |\\r\\n| `ai-os\\\\` | `~\\\\dev\\\\ai-os` | the whole vault, including code repos (minus `node_modules` and `.venv`) |\\r\\n| `dotfiles\\\\` | `.gitconfig`, `gh`, `gws`, `.config` | CLI logins and config |\\r\\n| `machine-info.json` | | which machine, when, which paths (restore uses this) |\\r\\n\\r\\nSecrets are included on purpose (decision 2026-09-11): the Drive is private and the goal is a\\r\\nzero-effort restore.\\r\\n\\r\\nAlso: the vault auto-commits and pushes to the private GitHub repo `vnjkoxpl-42905/ai-os`\\r\\n(history; `projects/`, `raw/`, `.env` stay out of GitHub, they are on Drive). And once a day a\\r\\nzip lands in `snapshots\\\\`, 30 kept, so one bad sync can never overwrite the only good copy.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Restore on a new laptop\\r\\nSee `README - START HERE.md` in the Drive folder. Short version: sign in to Google Drive,\\r\\ninstall Git + Node + Claude Code, run `restore.ps1` (Windows) or `restore.sh` (Mac).\\r\\n\\r\\n## Check it is running\\r\\n`LAST-BACKUP.txt` in the Drive folder shows the time of the last successful run.\\r\\nLogs: `logs\\\\backup-YYYY-MM-DD.log`. Run by hand: `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File backup.ps1`.\\r\\nRe-register the task: `install-task.ps1`.\\r\\n# backup.ps1 - mirror the whole Claude Code setup into Google Drive.\\r\\n# Runs every 10 minutes from a Scheduled Task (\\"Claude Code Backup\\").\\r\\n# Source of truth for this script: C:\\\\Users\\\\Joshu\\\\dev\\\\ai-os\\\\setup\\\\backup\\\\backup.ps1\\r\\n# Safe to run by hand:  powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File backup.ps1\\r\\n\\r\\n$ErrorActionPreference = \'Continue\'\\r\\n$Home_  = $env:USERPROFILE\\r\\n$Vault  = Join-Path $Home_ \'dev\\\\ai-os\'\\r\\n$Drive  = \'G:\\\\My Drive\\\\CLAUDE-CODE-BACKUP - START HERE\'\\r\\n$Cur    = Join-Path $Drive \'current\'\\r\\n$Snaps  = Join-Path $Drive \'snapshots\'\\r\\n$LogDir = Join-Path $Drive \'logs\'\\r\\n$Stamp  = Get-Date -Format \'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss\'\\r\\n\\r\\n# One run at a time: skip if another backup is still going.\\r\\n$mutex = New-Object System.Threading.Mutex($false, \'Global\\\\ClaudeCodeBackup\')\\r\\nif (-not $mutex.WaitOne(0)) { Write-Host \'Another backup is running - skipping\'; exit 0 }\\r\\n\\r\\nif (-not (Test-Path \'G:\\\\My Drive\')) { Write-Host \\"Google Drive not mounted at G:\\\\My Drive - skipping\\"; exit 1 }\\r\\nforeach ($d in $Cur, $Snaps, $LogDir) { New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $d | Out-Null }\\r\\n$Log = Join-Path $LogDir (\\"backup-\\" + (Get-Date -Format \'yyyy-MM-dd\') + \\".log\\")\\r\\n\\"=== $Stamp backup start ===\\" | Out-File -Append $Log\\r\\n\\r\\n# robocopy /MIR = make destination identical to source. /XJ skips junctions, /FFT tolerates Drive timestamps.\\r\\n$common = @(\'/MIR\',\'/XJ\',\'/FFT\',\'/R:1\',\'/W:1\',\'/NFL\',\'/NDL\',\'/NP\',\'/NJH\',\'/MT:8\')\\r\\nfunction Mirror($src, $dst, $xd) {\\r\\n    if (-not (Test-Path $src)) { \\"skip (missing): $src\\" | Out-File -Append $Log; return }\\r\\n    $args = @($src, $dst) + $common\\r\\n    if ($xd) { $args += \'/XD\'; $args += $xd }\\r\\n    $out = & robocopy @args 2>&1\\r\\n    $rc = $LASTEXITCODE   # robocopy: 0-7 = success codes, 8+ = failures\\r\\n    \\"$src -> $dst  rc=$rc\\" | Out-File -Append $Log\\r\\n    if ($rc -ge 8) { $out | Select-Object -Last 15 | Out-File -Append $Log }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n# 1. Claude Code: transcripts, skills, plugins, settings, memory, everything.\\r\\nMirror (Join-Path $Home_ \'.claude\') (Join-Path $Cur \'claude\') @()\\r\\n# 2. Claude Code global config (MCP servers, OAuth). Included on purpose - user decision 2026-09-11.\\r\\nCopy-Item (Join-Path $Home_ \'.claude.json\') (Join-Path $Cur \'claude.json\') -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue\\r\\n# 3. Codex CLI config + auth.\\r\\nMirror (Join-Path $Home_ \'.codex\') (Join-Path $Cur \'codex\') @(\'cache\')\\r\\n# 4. Superwhisper app data (modes, vocabulary, settings).\\r\\nMirror (Join-Path $env:LOCALAPPDATA \'Superwhisper\') (Join-Path $Cur \'superwhisper\') @()\\r\\n# 5. Keep the historical private-backup layout, with apps sourced outside the vault.\\r\\nMirror $Vault (Join-Path $Cur \'ai-os\') @(\'projects\',\'node_modules\',\'.venv\',\'venv\',\'.vite\',\'.turbo\',\'.next\',\'dist\',\'__pycache__\',\'.cache\',\'Main Conclusion \',\'Videos \')\\r\\n$Projects = Join-Path (Split-Path $Vault -Parent) \'ai-os-projects\'\\r\\nif (-not (Test-Path $Projects)) { $Projects = Join-Path $Vault \'projects\' }\\r\\nMirror $Projects (Join-Path $Cur \'ai-os\\\\projects\') @(\'node_modules\',\'.venv\',\'venv\',\'.vite\',\'.turbo\',\'.next\',\'dist\',\'__pycache__\',\'.cache\')\\r\\n# (Two empty raw/ folders whose names end in a space are excluded above: Google Drive cannot create them.)\\r\\n# 6. Other dotfiles that matter on a new machine.\\r\\n$dot = Join-Path $Cur \'dotfiles\'; New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $dot | Out-Null\\r\\nforeach ($f in \'.gitconfig\',\'.npmrc\',\'.bashrc\',\'.bash_profile\') {\\r\\n    $p = Join-Path $Home_ $f; if (Test-Path $p) { Copy-Item $p $dot -Force }\\r\\n}\\r\\nMirror (Join-Path $env:APPDATA \'gws\') (Join-Path $dot \'gws\') @()\\r\\nMirror (Join-Path $env:APPDATA \'gh\')  (Join-Path $dot \'gh\')  @()\\r\\nMirror (Join-Path $Home_ \'.config\')   (Join-Path $dot \'config\') @()\\r\\n\\r\\n# 7. Machine info so restore can rewrite paths.\\r\\n$cv = \'\'; try { $cv = (& claude --version 2>$null | Out-String).Trim() } catch {}\\r\\n@{ machine=$env:COMPUTERNAME; user=$env:USERNAME; home=$Home_; vault=$Vault; os=\'windows\'; backedUpAt=$Stamp; claudeVersion=$cv } |\\r\\n  ConvertTo-Json | Out-File (Join-Path $Cur \'machine-info.json\') -Encoding utf8\\r\\n\\"Last successful backup: $Stamp  (from $env:COMPUTERNAME)`r`nRestore instructions: see README - START HERE.md\\" |\\r\\n  Out-File (Join-Path $Drive \'LAST-BACKUP.txt\') -Encoding utf8\\r\\n\\r\\n# 8. Git: auto-commit + push the vault (private GitHub repo). Never blocks the Drive copy.\\r\\nif (Test-Path (Join-Path $Vault \'.git\')) {\\r\\n    Push-Location $Vault\\r\\n    & git add -A 2>&1 | Out-Null\\r\\n    $dirty = & git status --porcelain\\r\\n    if ($dirty) {\\r\\n        & git commit -q -m \\"auto-backup $Stamp\\" 2>&1 | Out-File -Append $Log\\r\\n        & git push -q 2>&1 | Out-File -Append $Log\\r\\n        \\"git: committed + pushed\\" | Out-File -Append $Log\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    Pop-Location\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n# 9. Daily snapshot zip (vault minus raw/projects + claude config), keep 30 days.\\r\\n$today = Get-Date -Format \'yyyy-MM-dd\'\\r\\n$zip = Join-Path $Snaps \\"snapshot-$today.zip\\"\\r\\nif (-not (Test-Path $zip)) {\\r\\n    $tmp = Join-Path $env:TEMP \\"cc-snap-$today\\"\\r\\n    Remove-Item $tmp -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue\\r\\n    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $tmp | Out-Null\\r\\n    & robocopy $Vault (Join-Path $tmp \'ai-os\') /MIR /XJ /R:0 /W:0 /NFL /NDL /NP /NJH /XD raw projects node_modules .venv tmp .obsidian .firecrawl | Out-Null\\r\\n    & robocopy (Join-Path $Home_ \'.claude\') (Join-Path $tmp \'claude\') /MIR /XJ /R:0 /W:0 /NFL /NDL /NP /NJH /XD plugins shell-snapshots | Out-Null\\r\\n    Copy-Item (Join-Path $Home_ \'.claude.json\') $tmp -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue\\r\\n    $localZip = Join-Path $env:TEMP \\"snapshot-$today.zip\\"\\r\\n    Remove-Item $localZip -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue\\r\\n    & \\"$env:SystemRoot\\\\System32\\\\tar.exe\\" -a -cf $localZip -C $tmp . 2>&1 | Out-File -Append $Log\\r\\n    if (Test-Path $localZip) { Move-Item $localZip $zip -Force } else { \\"snapshot FAILED: tar produced no file\\" | Out-File -Append $Log }\\r\\n    Remove-Item $tmp -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue\\r\\n    Get-ChildItem $Snaps -Filter \'snapshot-*.zip\' | Sort-Object Name -Descending | Select-Object -Skip 30 | Remove-Item -Force\\r\\n    \\"snapshot: $zip\\" | Out-File -Append $Log\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\"=== backup done ===\\" | Out-File -Append $Log\\r\\n\\r\\nName       Root\\r\\n----       ----\\r\\nC          C:\\\\\\r\\nTemp       C:\\\\Users\\\\Joshu\\\\AppData\\\\Local\\\\Temp\\\\\\r\\npython.exe \\r\\n7z.exe     \\r\\ngws.exe    \\r\\n\\r\\n"}'}]

text(await tools.apply_patch("*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/setup/backup/new-machine-backup.py\n+\"\"\"Non-deleting, incremental, private backup. Python 3.12+, Windows.\"\"\"\n+import argparse, hashlib, json, os, sqlite3, time, zipfile\n+from pathlib import Path\n+\n+p = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n+p.add_argument('--stage', required=True)\n+p.add_argument('--scope', choices=['core', 'all'], default='core')\n+a = p.parse_args()\n+home = Path.home()\n+stage = Path(a.stage).resolve()\n+stage.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n+stamp = time.strftime('%Y%m%d-%H%M%S')\n+run = stage / stamp\n+run.mkdir(exist_ok=False)\n+roots = {k: home / v for k, v in {\n+    'ai-os': 'dev/ai-os', 'projects': 'dev/ai-os-projects',\n+    'codex': '.codex', 'claude': '.claude', 'agents': '.agents',\n+    'config': '.config', 'superwhisper': 'AppData/Local/com.superwhisper.app',\n+    'codex-app': 'AppData/Roaming/Codex', 'obsidian': 'AppData/Roaming/obsidian',\n+    'vscode-user': 'AppData/Roaming/Code/User', 'gh': 'AppData/Roaming/gh',\n+    'gws': 'AppData/Roaming/gws',\n+}.items()}\n+if a.scope == 'all':\n+    roots.update({k: home / k for k in ['Documents', 'Downloads', 'Desktop', 'Pictures']})\n+excluded = {'node_modules', '.venv', 'venv', '__pycache__', '.cache', 'cache',\n+    'Cache', 'Code Cache', 'GPUCache', 'logs', 'log', 'tmp', '.tmp', '.sandbox',\n+    '.sandbox-bin', 'EBWebView', 'EBWebView_Main', 'models', 'Crashpad', 'browser',\n+    'backups', 'pre-restore', 'recovery-toolchain-20261006'}\n+previous = {}\n+for old in sorted(stage.glob('*/manifest.json'), reverse=True):\n+    doc = json.loads(old.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))\n+    if doc.get('scope') == a.scope and doc.get('complete'):\n+        previous = doc['files']; break\n+files, errors, skips = {}, [], []\n+archive = run / 'payload.zip'\n+def candidates():\n+    for label, root in roots.items():\n+        if not root.exists():\n+            skips.append({'path': str(root), 'reason': 'missing'}); continue\n+        for parent, dirs, names in os.walk(root, followlinks=False):\n+            base = Path(parent)\n+            dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d not in excluded\n+                and not (base / d).is_junction() and not (base / d).is_symlink()\n+                and not (base / d).resolve().is_relative_to(stage)\n+                and not (label == 'Documents' and d == 'work' and 'in-x20' in base.parts)]\n+            for name in names:\n+                source = base / name\n+                if source.is_symlink() or name.endswith(('-wal', '-shm', '.lock', '.tmp')):\n+                    continue\n+                if label == 'codex' and name.startswith('logs_'): continue\n+                yield label + '/' + source.relative_to(root).as_posix(), source\n+    for name in ['.claude.json', '.gitconfig', '.npmrc', '.bashrc']:\n+        source = home / name\n+        if source.exists(): yield 'dotfiles/' + name, source\n+\n+with zipfile.ZipFile(archive, 'x', compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED, compresslevel=1, allowZip64=True) as z:\n+    for key, source in candidates():\n+        try:\n+            st = source.stat()\n+            old = previous.get(key)\n+            isdb = source.suffix in ('.sqlite', '.db')\n+            if old and not isdb and old['size'] == st.st_size and old['mtime_ns'] == st.st_mtime_ns:\n+                files[key] = old; continue\n+            copyfrom = source\n+            if isdb:\n+                # backup() reads a consistent snapshot including committed WAL data.\n+                snap = run / 'sqlite-snapshots' / key\n+                snap.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n+                with sqlite3.connect(source.as_uri() + '?mode=ro', uri=True, timeout=5) as src:\n+                    with sqlite3.connect(snap) as dst:\n+                        src.backup(dst)\n+                        if dst.execute('PRAGMA quick_check').fetchone()[0] != 'ok':\n+                            raise RuntimeError('SQLite integrity check failed')\n+                copyfrom = snap\n+            digest = hashlib.sha256()\n+            with copyfrom.open('rb') as f, z.open(key, 'w', force_zip64=True) as out:\n+                while chunk := f.read(1024 * 1024):\n+                    digest.update(chunk); out.write(chunk)\n+            if not isdb and source.stat().st_mtime_ns != st.st_mtime_ns:\n+                raise RuntimeError('changed during capture; retry next run')\n+            files[key] = {'size': copyfrom.stat().st_size, 'mtime_ns': st.st_mtime_ns,\n+                'sha256': digest.hexdigest(), 'generation': stamp}\n+        except Exception as e:\n+            errors.append({'path': str(source), 'error': str(e)})\n+with zipfile.ZipFile(archive) as check:\n+    if check.testzip(): errors.append({'error': 'archive CRC validation failed'})\n+h = hashlib.sha256()\n+with archive.open('rb') as f:\n+    while chunk := f.read(1024 * 1024): h.update(chunk)\n+manifest = {'format': 1, 'generation': stamp, 'scope': a.scope, 'machine': os.environ.get('COMPUTERNAME'),\n+    'home': str(home), 'roots': {k: str(v) for k,v in roots.items()},\n+    'complete': not errors, 'files': files, 'errors': errors, 'skipped_roots': skips,\n+    'exclusions': sorted(excluded), 'archive_sha256': h.hexdigest(), 'archive_bytes': archive.stat().st_size}\n+(run / 'manifest.json').write_text(json.dumps(manifest, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')\n+(run / 'SHA256SUMS.txt').write_text(h.hexdigest() + '  payload.zip\\n', encoding='utf-8')\n+print(json.dumps({'generation':str(run), 'files':len(files), 'errors':len(errors), 'archive_bytes':archive.stat().st_size}))\n+raise SystemExit(1 if errors else 0)\n*** End Patch"));

text(await tools.apply_patch("*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/setup/backup/new-machine-restore.py\n+\"\"\"Materialize a selected generation into a NEW folder; never overwrite live data.\"\"\"\n+import argparse, hashlib, json, zipfile\n+from pathlib import Path\n+p = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n+p.add_argument('--generation', required=True)\n+p.add_argument('--output', required=True)\n+a = p.parse_args()\n+generation = Path(a.generation).resolve()\n+output = Path(a.output).resolve()\n+if output.exists(): raise SystemExit('Output must be a new folder, preventing overwrite.')\n+manifest = json.loads((generation / 'manifest.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))\n+if not manifest['complete']: raise SystemExit('Generation has capture errors; inspect manifest first.')\n+output.mkdir(parents=True)\n+archives = {}\n+for key, record in manifest['files'].items():\n+    target = (output / key).resolve()\n+    if not target.is_relative_to(output): raise SystemExit('Unsafe archive path')\n+    origin = record['generation']\n+    if origin not in archives:\n+        archives[origin] = zipfile.ZipFile(generation.parent / origin / 'payload.zip')\n+    target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n+    digest = hashlib.sha256()\n+    with archives[origin].open(key) as src, target.open('xb') as dst:\n+        while chunk := src.read(1024 * 1024):\n+            digest.update(chunk); dst.write(chunk)\n+    if digest.hexdigest() != record['sha256']: raise SystemExit('Checksum mismatch: ' + key)\n+for archive in archives.values(): archive.close()\n+print('Verified materialization complete:', len(manifest['files']), 'files in', output)\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/setup/backup/new-machine-README.md\n+# Replacement laptop backup and restore\n+\n+This workflow preserves dated generations and never deletes files, mirrors directories,\n+commits to Git, or changes live Codex databases. The older `backup.ps1` is not part of it.\n+\n+## What is included\n+\n+Core: the entire ai-os vault including `.env` and `raw`, sibling app repositories when\n+present, Codex/Claude chats and skills, global agents, CLI settings, Superwhisper recordings\n+and modes, Obsidian and editor user settings. SQLite uses a consistent read-only backup.\n+Personal scope (`--scope all`) additionally captures Documents, Downloads, Desktop and\n+Pictures. Apps themselves and downloaded models must be reinstalled. Account credentials\n+may be machine-bound, so reconnecting accounts can still be required.\n+\n+Caches, dependency directories, browser profiles, lock/WAL temporary files, model downloads,\n+and the old `Documents/Codex/2026-10-06/in-x20/work` recovery staging are excluded. Original\n+cloud backup archives are unchanged. Symlinks/junctions are not followed. Missing roots\n+and capture failures are recorded in the manifest. A failure prevents `complete=true`.\n+\n+## Capture\n+\n+```powershell\n+python C:\\Users\\Joshu\\dev\\ai-os\\setup\\backup\\new-machine-backup.py --stage C:\\Users\\Joshu\\LaptopBackups\\2026-10-07 --scope core\n+```\n+\n+Use `--scope all` for personal files too. Repeated captures of the same scope store only\n+new or changed files. Keep ALL generation folders: the manifest points to the generation\n+that contains each unchanged file. Never upload only the most recent incremental folder.\n+The script validates archive CRCs and writes SHA256SUMS, full file checksums, explicit\n+errors and the source-to-restore mapping. Active files changed during capture are errors\n+and should be recaptured; backups do not require closing Codex.\n+\n+## Private Google Drive\n+\n+Upload every generation folder and these scripts to a private Drive folder using the\n+verified account. Never use GitHub for these archives because they include secrets and\n+personal files. A local completed archive is NOT proof of cloud backup. Record each\n+uploaded file ID, size and Drive MD5 checksum, and compare it with the local file.\n+No automatic scheduled task is enabled until a successful cloud upload is verified.\n+\n+## Restore onto another Windows computer\n+\n+1. Download all generations and install Python 3.12 or newer.\n+2. Materialize the desired generation into a NEW folder:\n+\n+```powershell\n+python new-machine-restore.py --generation C:\\Backups\\20261007-113510 --output C:\\RestoredLaptop\n+```\n+\n+3. The restore verifies every extracted file's SHA256. Copy `ai-os` to your new user's\n+   `dev/ai-os`. Use manifest `roots` to map the other folders to the equivalent paths\n+   under your new Windows user. Preserve current data before merging or replacement.\n+4. Close the corresponding app before applying its database files. Reinstall apps and\n+   reconnect accounts. Different usernames require reviewing saved absolute paths.\n+\n+This is a portable data backup, not a bootable Windows disk image. Apps, Windows licenses,\n+Personal Vault files never captured on the old machine and unreadable cloud files cannot\n+be recreated from it.\n*** End Patch"));

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 1.2 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"f8a0c7","wall_time_seconds":0.4308858,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":1027,"output":"---\\r\\nname: gws-drive-upload\\r\\ndescription: \\"Google Drive: Upload a file with automatic metadata.\\"\\r\\nmetadata:\\r\\n  version: 0.22.5\\r\\n  openclaw:\\r\\n    category: \\"productivity\\"\\r\\n    requires:\\r\\n      bins:\\r\\n        - gws\\r\\n    cliHelp: \\"gws drive +upload --help\\"\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\n# drive +upload\\r\\n\\r\\n> **PREREQUISITE:** Read `../gws-shared/SKILL.md` for auth, global flags, and security rules. If missing, run `gws generate-skills` to create it.\\r\\n\\r\\nUpload a file with automatic metadata\\r\\n\\r\\n## Usage\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\ngws drive +upload <file>\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n## Flags\\r\\n\\r\\n| Flag | Required | Default | Description |\\r\\n|------|----------|---------|-------------|\\r\\n| `<file>` | ✓ | — | Path to file to upload |\\r\\n| `--parent` | — | — | Parent folder ID |\\r\\n| `--name` | — | — | Target filename (defaults to source filename) |\\r\\n\\r\\n## Examples\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\ngws drive +upload ./report.pdf\\r\\ngws drive +upload ./report.pdf --parent FOLDER_ID\\r\\ngws drive +upload ./data.csv --name \'Sales Data.csv\'\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n## Tips\\r\\n\\r\\n- MIME type is detected automatically.\\r\\n- Filename is inferred from the local path unless --name is given.\\r\\n\\r\\n> [!CAUTION]\\r\\n> This is a **write** command — confirm with the user before executing.\\r\\n\\r\\n## See Also\\r\\n\\r\\n- [gws-shared](../gws-shared/SKILL.md) — Global flags and auth\\r\\n- [gws-drive](../gws-drive/SKILL.md) — All manage files, folders, and shared drives commands\\r\\n---\\r\\nname: gws-shared\\r\\ndescription: \\"gws CLI: Shared patterns for authentication, global flags, and output formatting.\\"\\r\\nmetadata:\\r\\n  version: 0.22.1\\r\\n  openclaw:\\r\\n    category: \\"productivity\\"\\r\\n    requires:\\r\\n      bins:\\r\\n        - gws\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\n# gws — Shared Reference\\r\\n\\r\\n## Installation\\r\\n\\r\\nThe `gws` binary must be on `$PATH`. See the project README for install options.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Authentication\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\n# Browser-based OAuth (interactive)\\r\\ngws auth login\\r\\n\\r\\n# Service Account\\r\\nexport GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS=/path/to/key.json\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n## Global Flags\\r\\n\\r\\n| Flag | Description |\\r\\n|------|-------------|\\r\\n| `--format <FORMAT>` | Output format: `json` (default), `table`, `yaml`, `csv` |\\r\\n| `--dry-run` | Validate locally without calling the API |\\r\\n| `--sanitize <TEMPLATE>` | Screen responses through Model Armor |\\r\\n\\r\\n## CLI Syntax\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\ngws <service> <resource> [sub-resource] <method> [flags]\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n### Method Flags\\r\\n\\r\\n| Flag | Description |\\r\\n|------|-------------|\\r\\n| `--params \'{\\"key\\": \\"val\\"}\'` | URL/query parameters |\\r\\n| `--json \'{\\"key\\": \\"val\\"}\'` | Request body |\\r\\n| `-o, --output <PATH>` | Save binary responses to file |\\r\\n| `--upload <PATH>` | Upload file content (multipart) |\\r\\n| `--page-all` | Auto-paginate (NDJSON output) |\\r\\n| `--page-limit <N>` | Max pages when using --page-all (default: 10) |\\r\\n| `--page-delay <MS>` | Delay between pages in ms (default: 100) |\\r\\n\\r\\n## Security Rules\\r\\n\\r\\n- **Never** output secrets (API keys, tokens) directly\\r\\n- **Always** confirm with user before executing write/delete commands\\r\\n- Prefer `--dry-run` for destructive operations\\r\\n- Use `--sanitize` for PII/content safety screening\\r\\n\\r\\n## Shell Tips\\r\\n\\r\\n- **zsh `!` expansion:** Sheet ranges like `Sheet1!A1` contain `!` which zsh interprets as history expansion. Use double quotes with escaped inner quotes instead of single quotes:\\r\\n  ```bash\\r\\n  # WRONG (zsh will mangle the !)\\r\\n  gws sheets +read --spreadsheet ID --range \'Sheet1!A1:D10\'\\r\\n\\r\\n  # CORRECT\\r\\n  gws sheets +read --spreadsheet ID --range \\"Sheet1!A1:D10\\"\\r\\n  ```\\r\\n- **JSON with double quotes:** Wrap `--params` and `--json` values in single quotes so the shell does not interpret the inner double quotes:\\r\\n  ```bash\\r\\n  gws drive files list --params \'{\\"pageSize\\": 5}\'\\r\\n  ```\\r\\n\\r\\n## Community & Feedback Etiquette\\r\\n\\r\\n- Encourage agents/users to star the repository when they find the project useful: `https://github.com/googleworkspace/cli`\\r\\n- For bugs or feature requests, direct users to open issues in the repository: `https://github.com/googleworkspace/cli/issues`\\r\\n- Before creating a new issue, **always** search existing issues and feature requests first\\r\\n- If a matching issue already exists, add context by commenting on the existing thread instead of creating a duplicate\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"b1397e","wall_time_seconds":0.3505876,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":338,"output":"Creates a file. For more information, see Create and manage files.\\n\\nUsage: gws files create [OPTIONS]\\n\\nOptions:\\n      --params <JSON>               JSON string for URL/Query parameters\\n      --sanitize <TEMPLATE>         Sanitize API responses through a Model Armor template. Requires cloud-platform scope. Format: projects/PROJECT/locations/LOCATION/templates/TEMPLATE. Also reads GWS_SANITIZE_TEMPLATE env var.\\n      --dry-run                     Validate the request locally without sending it to the API\\n  -o, --output <PATH>               Output file path for binary responses\\n      --format <FORMAT>             Output format: json (default), table, yaml, csv\\n      --json <JSON>                 JSON string for the request body\\n      --upload <PATH>               Local file path to upload as media content (multipart upload)\\n      --upload-content-type <MIME>  MIME type of the uploaded file content (e.g. text/markdown). If omitted, detected from file extension or metadata mimeType\\n      --page-all                    Auto-paginate through all results, outputting one JSON line per page (NDJSON)\\n      --page-limit <N>              Maximum number of pages to fetch when using --page-all (default: 10)\\n      --page-delay <MS>             Delay in milliseconds between page fetches (default: 100)\\n  -h, --help                        Print help\\n"}'}]

text(await tools.apply_patch("*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/setup/backup/new-machine-cloud.py\n+\"\"\"Upload immutable generation chunks to private Drive; verify MD5 and size.\"\"\"\n+import argparse, concurrent.futures, hashlib, json, shutil, subprocess\n+from pathlib import Path\n+p=argparse.ArgumentParser()\n+p.add_argument('--generation',required=True)\n+p.add_argument('--parent',default='1UtlkMAzoo4dMMWgj2kTw9U4QR9p-sAUf')\n+a=p.parse_args()\n+generation=Path(a.generation).resolve()\n+def gws(*args):\n+    r=subprocess.run(['gws',*args],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=300)\n+    if r.returncode: raise RuntimeError(r.stderr[:1000] or r.stdout[:1000])\n+    return json.loads(r.stdout)\n+about=gws('drive','about','get','--params',json.dumps({'fields':'user(emailAddress)'}))\n+if about['user']['emailAddress'].lower()!='contact@aspiringattorneys.com':\n+    raise SystemExit('Wrong Google account; upload refused')\n+manifest=json.loads((generation/'manifest.json').read_text())\n+if not manifest['complete']: raise SystemExit('Incomplete capture; upload refused')\n+def folder(name,parent):\n+    q=\"trashed = false and '%s' in parents and name = '%s' and mimeType = 'application/vnd.google-apps.folder'\"%(parent,name)\n+    found=gws('drive','files','list','--params',json.dumps({'q':q,'fields':'files(id,name)','pageSize':100}))['files']\n+    if len(found)>1: raise RuntimeError('Duplicate destination folder: '+name)\n+    if found: return found[0]['id']\n+    return gws('drive','files','create','--json',json.dumps({'name':name,'parents':[parent],'mimeType':'application/vnd.google-apps.folder'}),'--params',json.dumps({'fields':'id'}))['id']\n+root=folder('Replacement Laptop Backups',a.parent)\n+destination=folder(generation.name,root)\n+parts=generation/'cloud-parts'\n+parts.mkdir(exist_ok=True)\n+records=[]\n+with (generation/'payload.zip').open('rb') as src:\n+    n=1\n+    while chunk:=src.read(128*1024*1024):\n+        target=parts/('payload.zip.part%04d'%n)\n+        md5=hashlib.md5(chunk).hexdigest(); sha=hashlib.sha256(chunk).hexdigest()\n+        if target.exists():\n+            if hashlib.sha256(target.read_bytes()).hexdigest()!=sha: raise RuntimeError('Existing part differs')\n+        else: target.write_bytes(chunk)\n+        records.append({'name':target.name,'bytes':len(chunk),'md5':md5,'sha256':sha}); n+=1\n+(generation/'parts-manifest.json').write_text(json.dumps({'archive_sha256':manifest['archive_sha256'],'parts':records},indent=2))\n+here=Path(__file__).parent\n+for name in ['new-machine-backup.py','new-machine-restore.py','new-machine-cloud.py','new-machine-README.md']:\n+    if not (generation/name).exists(): shutil.copyfile(here/name,generation/name)\n+targets=[parts/r['name'] for r in records]+[generation/n for n in ['manifest.json','parts-manifest.json','SHA256SUMS.txt','new-machine-backup.py','new-machine-restore.py','new-machine-cloud.py','new-machine-README.md']]\n+def upload(path):\n+    h=hashlib.md5()\n+    with path.open('rb') as f:\n+        while chunk:=f.read(1024*1024): h.update(chunk)\n+    digest=h.hexdigest(); size=path.stat().st_size\n+    params={'q':\"trashed=false and '%s' in parents and name='%s'\"%(destination,path.name),'fields':'files(id,name,size,md5Checksum)','pageSize':100}\n+    existing=gws('drive','files','list','--params',json.dumps(params))['files']\n+    match=[r for r in existing if r.get('md5Checksum')==digest and int(r.get('size',-1))==size]\n+    if match: result=match[0]\n+    else:\n+        if existing: raise RuntimeError('Same-name cloud file differs: '+path.name)\n+        # Unknown upload outcome: subsequent rerun queries checksum before retry.\n+        result=gws('drive','files','create','--json',json.dumps({'name':path.name,'parents':[destination]}),'--upload',str(path),'--params',json.dumps({'fields':'id,name,size,md5Checksum'}))\n+    verified=gws('drive','files','get','--params',json.dumps({'fileId':result['id'],'fields':'id,name,size,md5Checksum'}))\n+    if verified.get('md5Checksum')!=digest or int(verified.get('size',-1))!=size: raise RuntimeError('Cloud checksum failed: '+path.name)\n+    print('Verified cloud file:',path.name,flush=True)\n+    return verified\n+verified=[]\n+with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as pool:\n+    for result in pool.map(upload,targets): verified.append(result)\n+receipt={'complete':True,'account':'contact@aspiringattorneys.com','root_id':root,'folder_id':destination,'generation':generation.name,'files':verified}\n+(generation/'CLOUD-VERIFIED.json').write_text(json.dumps(receipt,indent=2))\n+print(json.dumps({'complete':True,'folder_id':destination,'files':len(verified)}))\n*** End Patch"));

text(await tools.apply_patch("*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/setup/backup/new-machine-restore.py\n@@\n     origin = record['generation']\n     if origin not in archives:\n-        archives[origin] = zipfile.ZipFile(generation.parent / origin / 'payload.zip')\n+        origin_dir = generation.parent / origin\n+        payload = origin_dir / 'payload.zip'\n+        if not payload.exists():\n+            parts = json.loads((origin_dir / 'parts-manifest.json').read_text())\n+            payload = origin_dir / 'reassembled-payload.zip'\n+            if payload.exists(): raise SystemExit('Reassembled file exists; verify it before retrying.')\n+            full = hashlib.sha256()\n+            with payload.open('xb') as dst:\n+                for part in parts['parts']:\n+                    path = origin_dir / part['name']\n+                    if not path.exists(): path = origin_dir / 'cloud-parts' / part['name']\n+                    digest_part = hashlib.sha256()\n+                    with path.open('rb') as src:\n+                        while chunk := src.read(1024 * 1024):\n+                            full.update(chunk); digest_part.update(chunk); dst.write(chunk)\n+                    if digest_part.hexdigest() != part['sha256']: raise SystemExit('Part checksum mismatch')\n+            if full.hexdigest() != parts['archive_sha256']: raise SystemExit('Whole archive checksum mismatch')\n+        archives[origin] = zipfile.ZipFile(payload)\n*** Update File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/setup/backup/new-machine-README.md\n@@\n-Upload every generation folder and these scripts to a private Drive folder using the\n+Cloud uploader splits archives into numbered 128 MiB parts. The restore script reassembles\n+them, verifies each part and the complete archive, then verifies extracted files. Download\n+every cloud generation folder into one parent directory, keeping generation folder names.\n+\n+Upload every generation folder and these scripts to a private Drive folder using the\n*** End Patch"));

text(await tools.apply_patch("*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/setup/backup/new-machine-run.py\n+\"\"\"Scheduled private backup coordinator. No source changes or pruning.\"\"\"\n+import json, msvcrt, subprocess, sys, time\n+from pathlib import Path\n+here=Path(__file__).resolve().parent\n+stage=Path.home()/'LaptopBackups'/'2026-10-07'\n+stage.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)\n+lock=(stage/'scheduled.lock').open('a+b')\n+try: msvcrt.locking(lock.fileno(),msvcrt.LK_NBLCK,1)\n+except OSError: raise SystemExit('Another scheduled run is active')\n+log=stage/('run-'+time.strftime('%Y%m%d-%H%M%S')+'.log')\n+with log.open('x',encoding='utf-8') as out:\n+    for scope in ['core','personal']:\n+        subprocess.run([sys.executable,str(here/'new-machine-backup.py'),'--stage',str(stage),'--scope',scope],stdout=out,stderr=out,check=True)\n+    for generation in sorted(stage.iterdir()):\n+        if not generation.is_dir() or not (generation/'manifest.json').exists(): continue\n+        manifest=json.loads((generation/'manifest.json').read_text())\n+        if not manifest['complete'] or (generation/'CLOUD-VERIFIED.json').exists(): continue\n+        subprocess.run([sys.executable,str(here/'new-machine-cloud.py'),'--generation',str(generation)],stdout=out,stderr=out,check=True)\n+    (stage/'LATEST-CLOUD-SUCCESS.txt').write_text(time.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')+'\\n'+str(log),encoding='utf-8')\n+print('All complete generations verified on private Drive')\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/setup/backup/new-machine-install-task.ps1\n+$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'\n+$Stage = Join-Path $env:USERPROFILE 'LaptopBackups\\2026-10-07'\n+$Receipts = @(Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Stage -Filter CLOUD-VERIFIED.json -Recurse -File)\n+if ($Receipts.Count -eq 0) { throw 'No cloud verification receipt exists. Task registration refused.' }\n+$PythonPath = (Get-Command python -ErrorAction Stop).Source\n+$RunnerPath = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'new-machine-run.py'\n+$TaskName = 'Private Laptop Backup - Replacement'\n+if (Get-ScheduledTask -TaskName $TaskName -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue) { throw 'Task already exists; inspect before changing it.' }\n+$Action = New-ScheduledTaskAction -Execute $PythonPath -Argument ('\"' + $RunnerPath + '\"') -WorkingDirectory $PSScriptRoot\n+$Triggers = @(New-ScheduledTaskTrigger -Daily -At '7:00PM'; New-ScheduledTaskTrigger -AtLogOn -User ([System.Security.Principal.WindowsIdentity]::GetCurrent().Name))\n+$Principal = New-ScheduledTaskPrincipal -UserId ([System.Security.Principal.WindowsIdentity]::GetCurrent().Name) -LogonType Interactive -RunLevel Limited\n+$Settings = New-ScheduledTaskSettingsSet -MultipleInstances IgnoreNew -ExecutionTimeLimit (New-TimeSpan -Hours 4) -AllowStartIfOnBatteries -DontStopIfGoingOnBatteries -StartWhenAvailable\n+Register-ScheduledTask -TaskName $TaskName -Action $Action -Trigger $Triggers -Principal $Principal -Settings $Settings -Description 'Non-deleting dated private Google Drive backup; explicit account and cloud checksum verification; never commits or prunes.' | Out-Null\n+Write-Output 'Registered daily 19:00 local and at logon. Old recovery tasks are untouched.'\n*** End Patch"));

text(await tools.apply_patch("*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/setup/backup/new-machine-upload-after-capture.py\n+\"\"\"Wait for the already-running hidden capture using a Windows process handle.\"\"\"\n+import argparse, ctypes, json, subprocess, sys\n+from pathlib import Path\n+p=argparse.ArgumentParser(); p.add_argument('--pid',type=int,required=True); p.add_argument('--log',required=True)\n+a=p.parse_args()\n+kernel=ctypes.WinDLL('kernel32',use_last_error=True)\n+kernel.OpenProcess.restype=ctypes.c_void_p\n+kernel.WaitForSingleObject.argtypes=[ctypes.c_void_p,ctypes.c_ulong]\n+kernel.CloseHandle.argtypes=[ctypes.c_void_p]\n+handle=kernel.OpenProcess(0x00100000,False,a.pid)\n+if handle:\n+    result=kernel.WaitForSingleObject(handle,3600000); kernel.CloseHandle(handle)\n+    if result!=0: raise SystemExit('Capture wait failed or exceeded one hour')\n+lines=Path(a.log).read_text().splitlines()\n+if not lines: raise SystemExit('Capture produced no completion summary')\n+summary=json.loads(lines[-1])\n+if summary.get('errors')!=0: raise SystemExit('Capture errors; no cloud success claimed')\n+subprocess.run([sys.executable,str(Path(__file__).with_name('new-machine-cloud.py')),'--generation',summary['generation']],check=True)\n*** End Patch"));

Missing roots\n@@\n 4. Close the corresponding app before applying its database files. Reinstall apps and\n    reconnect accounts. Different usernames require reviewing saved absolute paths.\n+\n+`system/machine-info.json` preserves the user's Windows environment registry values,\n+including `CLAUDE_CODE_GIT_BASH_PATH`. Review paths before restoring environment values\n+on another computer. Install Git for Windows first and point that variable at its\n+`bin/bash.exe` if Claude Code requires it. Avoid replacing the new computer's entire PATH.\n+\n+The replacement backup runs daily at 19:00 local and at logon once cloud verification\n+has succeeded and `new-machine-install-task.ps1` has registered it. It captures core and\n+personal changes, uploads every complete pending generation and verifies Drive MD5/size.\n+Failures leave a dated local log and no success marker. Check Windows Task Scheduler's\n+`Private Laptop Backup - Replacement` result and local `LATEST-CLOUD-SUCCESS.txt`.\n*** End Patch"));

text(await tools.apply_patch("*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/setup/backup/new-machine-inventory.py\n+\"\"\"Read-only scope counts; no content reads or credential output.\"\"\"\n+import ast, json, os\n+from pathlib import Path\n+tree=ast.parse(Path(__file__).with_name('new-machine-backup.py').read_text())\n+excluded=next(ast.literal_eval(n.value) for n in tree.body if isinstance(n,ast.Assign)\n+    and any(isinstance(t,ast.Name) and t.id=='excluded' for t in n.targets))\n+roots=['dev/ai-os','dev/ai-os-projects','.codex','.claude','.agents','.config',\n+    'AppData/Local/com.superwhisper.app','AppData/Roaming/Codex','AppData/Roaming/obsidian',\n+    'AppData/Roaming/Code/User','AppData/Roaming/gh','AppData/Roaming/gws']\n+counts={}; totalbytes=0; errors=[]\n+for rel in roots:\n+    count=0\n+    for parent,dirs,files in os.walk(Path.home()/rel,onerror=lambda e:errors.append(str(e))):\n+        base=Path(parent)\n+        dirs[:]=[d for d in dirs if d not in excluded and not (base/d).is_symlink() and not (base/d).is_junction()]\n+        for name in files:\n+            if name.endswith(('-wal','-shm','.lock','.tmp')): continue\n+            if rel=='.codex' and name.startswith('logs_'): continue\n+            count+=1\n+            try: totalbytes+=(base/name).stat().st_size\n+            except OSError as error:errors.append(str(error))\n+    counts[rel]=count\n+print(json.dumps({'counts':counts,'total_files':sum(counts.values()),'source_bytes':totalbytes,'traversal_errors':errors}))\n*** End Patch"));

text(await tools.apply_patch("*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/setup/backup/new-machine-cloud-status.py\n+\"\"\"Read-only cloud progress summary; emits only generated backup metadata.\"\"\"\n+import json, subprocess\n+root='1vQ_Xm9GqDd7SlahE4sp6znQKiYkW8xOe'\n+def get(params):\n+    return json.loads(subprocess.run(['gws','drive','files','list','--params',json.dumps(params)],capture_output=True,text=True,check=True).stdout)['files']\n+folders=get({'q':\"trashed=false and '%s' in parents\"%root,'fields':'files(id,name)','pageSize':100})\n+for folder in folders:\n+    files=get({'q':\"trashed=false and '%s' in parents\"%folder['id'],'fields':'files(name,size)','pageSize':100})\n+    print(json.dumps({'generation':folder['name'],'files':len(files),\n+        'bytes':sum(int(f.get('size',0)) for f in files),\n+        'helper_names':[f['name'] for f in files if not f['name'].startswith('payload.zip')]}))\n*** End Patch"));
