compare-and-swap refused')\n+os.replace(temporary,receipt_path)\n*** Update File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/setup/backup/new-machine-run.py\n@@\n runtime=here/'new-machine-runtime.json'\n if runtime.exists(): os.environ['GWS_EXECUTABLE']=json.loads(runtime.read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig'))['gws']\n+def verified_receipt(generation):\n+    try:\n+        receipt=json.loads((generation/'CLOUD-VERIFIED.json').read_text())\n+        return receipt.get('complete') is True and receipt.get('generation')==generation.name and receipt.get('account')=='contact@aspiringattorneys.com' and bool(receipt.get('files')) and all(r.get('id') and len(r.get('md5Checksum',''))==32 and int(r.get('size',-1))>=0 for r in receipt['files'])\n+    except (OSError,ValueError,TypeError): return False\n@@\n-        if not manifest['complete'] or (generation/'CLOUD-VERIFIED.json').exists(): continue\n+        if not manifest['complete']: continue\n+        dependencies={r['generation'] for r in manifest['files'].values()}|{generation.name}\n+        if all(verified_receipt(stage/dep) for dep in dependencies): continue\n@@\n         if cloud.returncode: failures.append('Cloud upload failed: '+generation.name)\n+        elif not all(verified_receipt(stage/dep) for dep in dependencies): failures.append('Cloud receipt chain failed: '+generation.name)\n*** Update File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/setup/backup/new-machine-install-task.ps1\n@@\n $Receipts = @(Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Stage -Filter CLOUD-VERIFIED.json -Recurse -File)\n if ($Receipts.Count -eq 0) { throw 'No cloud verification receipt exists. Task registration refused.' }\n-$VerifiedScopes = @($Receipts | ForEach-Object { $Manifest = Get-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $_.DirectoryName 'manifest.json') -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json; if ($Manifest.complete) { $Manifest.scope } })\n+function Test-CloudReceipt([string]$GenerationDirectory) {\n+    try {\n+        $Receipt = Get-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $GenerationDirectory 'CLOUD-VERIFIED.json') -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json\n+        if (-not $Receipt.complete -or $Receipt.account -ne 'contact@aspiringattorneys.com' -or $Receipt.generation -ne (Split-Path $GenerationDirectory -Leaf) -or $Receipt.files.Count -lt 1) { return $false }\n+        foreach ($File in $Receipt.files) { if (-not $File.id -or $File.md5Checksum -notmatch '^[0-9a-f]{32}$' -or [long]$File.size -lt 0) { return $false } }\n+        return $true\n+    } catch { return $false }\n+}\n+$VerifiedScopes = @($Receipts | ForEach-Object {\n+    $Directory = $_.DirectoryName\n+    $Manifest = Get-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $Directory 'manifest.json') -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json\n+    $ChainValid = Test-CloudReceipt $Directory\n+    if ($Manifest.complete -and $ChainValid) {\n+        $Dependencies = @($Manifest.files.PSObject.Properties.Value.generation | Select-Object -Unique)\n+        foreach ($Dependency in $Dependencies) { if (-not (Test-CloudReceipt (Join-Path $Stage $Dependency))) { $ChainValid = $false } }\n+        if ($ChainValid) { $Manifest.scope }\n+    }\n+})\n*** End Patch"));

text(await tools.apply_patch("*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/setup/backup/new-machine-proof-checks.py\n+\"\"\"Verify receipt validation in isolation; never invokes cloud mutations.\"\"\"\n+import ast, json, time\n+from pathlib import Path\n+here=Path(__file__).parent\n+root=Path.home()/'LaptopBackups'/('receipt-test-'+str(time.time_ns()))\n+base=root/'base'; final=root/'final'\n+base.mkdir(parents=True); final.mkdir()\n+for script in ['new-machine-cloud.py','new-machine-run.py']:\n+    tree=ast.parse((here/script).read_text())\n+    function=next(n for n in tree.body if isinstance(n,ast.FunctionDef) and n.name=='verified_receipt')\n+    namespace={'json':json}\n+    exec(compile(ast.Module(body=[function],type_ignores=[]),script,'exec'),namespace)\n+    check=namespace['verified_receipt']\n+    (base/'CLOUD-VERIFIED.json').write_text('')\n+    assert not check(base)\n+    (base/'CLOUD-VERIFIED.json').write_text('{broken')\n+    assert not check(base)\n+    for directory in [base,final]:\n+        receipt={'complete':True,'generation':directory.name,'account':'contact@aspiringattorneys.com',\n+            'files':[{'id':'synthetic-file-id','md5Checksum':'0'*32,'size':'1'}]}\n+        (directory/'CLOUD-VERIFIED.json').write_text(json.dumps(receipt))\n+    assert all(check(directory) for directory in [base,final])\n+    receipt['account']='wrong-account@example.invalid'\n+    (final/'CLOUD-VERIFIED.json').write_text(json.dumps(receipt))\n+    assert not all(check(directory) for directory in [base,final])\n+    print('PASS',script,'rejects empty/malformed/wrong-account receipts and accepts valid dependency chain')\n*** End Patch"));

They are separate scopes; restoring only personal does not\n+   restore the second brain. Keep their older dependency generations too.\n+2. Materialize the selected core generation into one NEW folder, then materialize the\n+   selected personal generation into another NEW folder:\n@@\n-python new-machine-restore.py --generation C:\\Backups\\20261007-113510 --output C:\\RestoredLaptop\n+python new-machine-restore.py --generation C:\\Backups\\CORE_TIMESTAMP --output C:\\RestoredCore\n+python new-machine-restore.py --generation C:\\Backups\\PERSONAL_TIMESTAMP --output C:\\RestoredPersonal\n@@\n-3. Copy `RestoredCore/ai-os` to your new user's\n*** End Patch"));

text(await tools.apply_patch("*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/setup/backup/new-machine-publish-index.py\n+\"\"\"Publish an immutable index selecting only fully verified core and personal chains.\"\"\"\n+import hashlib, json, os, shutil, subprocess, time, uuid\n+from pathlib import Path\n+stage=Path.home()/'LaptopBackups'/'2026-10-07'\n+root_id='1vQ_Xm9GqDd7SlahE4sp6znQKiYkW8xOe'\n+binary=os.environ.get('GWS_EXECUTABLE') or shutil.which('gws')\n+if not binary: raise SystemExit('gws unavailable')\n+hidden=subprocess.CREATE_NO_WINDOW if os.name=='nt' else 0\n+def call(*args):\n+    result=subprocess.run([binary,*args],capture_output=True,text=True,cwd=stage,timeout=300,creationflags=hidden)\n+    if result.returncode: raise RuntimeError('gws index publication request failed')\n+    return json.loads(result.stdout)\n+if call('drive','about','get','--params',json.dumps({'fields':'user(emailAddress)'}))['user']['emailAddress'].lower()!='contact@aspiringattorneys.com':\n+    raise SystemExit('Wrong Google account')\n+def proof(directory):\n+    try:\n+        receipt=json.loads((directory/'CLOUD-VERIFIED.json').read_text())\n+        if receipt.get('complete') is not True or receipt.get('account')!='contact@aspiringattorneys.com' or receipt.get('generation')!=directory.name: return None\n+        if not receipt.get('files') or not all(f.get('id') and len(f.get('md5Checksum',''))==32 for f in receipt['files']): return None\n+        return receipt\n+    except (OSError,ValueError,TypeError): return None\n+selected={}\n+for path in sorted(stage.glob('*/manifest.json'),reverse=True):\n+    manifest=json.loads(path.read_text())\n+    scope=manifest['scope']\n+    if scope not in ['core','personal'] or scope in selected or not manifest['complete']: continue\n+    dependencies={r['generation'] for r in manifest['files'].values()}|{path.parent.name}\n+    if not all(proof(stage/dependency) for dependency in dependencies): continue\n+    receipt=proof(path.parent)\n+    selected[scope]={'generation':path.parent.name,'folder_id':receipt['folder_id'],\n+        'files':len(manifest['files']),'archive_bytes':manifest['archive_bytes'],\n+        'dependencies':sorted(dependencies)}\n+if set(selected)!= {'core','personal'}: raise SystemExit('Both verified backup scopes are required')\n+stamp=time.strftime('%Y%m%d-%H%M%S')+'-'+uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]\n+index=stage/('Restore-Index-'+stamp+'.json')\n+index.write_text(json.dumps({'created':time.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S'),'account':'contact@aspiringattorneys.com','root_folder_id':root_id,'scopes':selected},indent=2))\n+guide=stage/('Restore-Guide-'+stamp+'.md')\n+guide.write_text('''# Restore the verified replacement laptop backup\n+\n+Download the generation folders listed below from this private folder:\n+https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/'''+root_id+'''\n+\n+Keep every generation folder name unchanged inside one local folder, such as C:\\\\Backups.\n+Download every dependency listed in the index, including partial historical generation\n+folders: their verified archive files are required by the final complete manifest.\n+Install Python 3.12 or newer. Use new-machine-restore.py from the selected core folder.\n+\n+```powershell\n+python C:\\\\Backups\\\\'''+selected['core']['generation']+'''\\\\new-machine-restore.py --generation C:\\\\Backups\\\\'''+selected['core']['generation']+''' --output C:\\\\RestoredCore\n+python C:\\\\Backups\\\\'''+selected['core']['generation']+'''\\\\new-machine-restore.py --generation C:\\\\Backups\\\\'''+selected['personal']['generation']+''' --output C:\\\\RestoredPersonal\n+```\n+\n+Both output folders must be new. The script verifies parts, reassembles archives and\n+verifies every restored file SHA256. Map RestoredCore's ai-os to your new user's dev/ai-os;\n+use manifest roots for Codex, Claude, Superwhisper and the other folders. Apply database\n+files only while the corresponding app is closed. Copy personal folders separately.\n+Review saved user environment paths before applying them, especially Git Bash and PATH.\n+Install apps and reconnect accounts when Windows-bound credentials cannot be reused.\n+\n+The index selects only generations whose whole dependency chain was cloud verified.\n+Use the newest Restore-Index and matching Restore-Guide in the root folder, rather than\n+selecting an in-flight generation solely by its timestamp. No files are deleted by these\n+scripts. No credential or personal file is sent to GitHub.\n+''',encoding='utf-8')\n+remote=[]\n+for path in [index,guide]:\n+    digest=hashlib.md5(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest(); size=path.stat().st_size\n+    params={'q':\"trashed=false and '%s' in parents and name='%s'\"%(root_id,path.name),'fields':'files(id,name,size,md5Checksum)','pageSize':100}\n+    found=call('drive','files','list','--params',json.dumps(params))['files']\n+    matching=[f for f in found if f.get('md5Checksum')==digest and int(f.get('size',-1))==size]\n+    result=matching[0] if matching else call('drive','files','create','--json',json.dumps({'name':path.name,'parents':[root_id]}),'--upload',str(path),'--params',json.dumps({'fields':'id,name,size,md5Checksum'}))\n+    verified=call('drive','files','get','--params',json.dumps({'fileId':result['id'],'fields':'id,name,size,md5Checksum'}))\n+    if verified.get('md5Checksum')!=digest or int(verified.get('size',-1))!=size: raise SystemExit('Index upload checksum mismatch')\n+    remote.append(verified)\n+print(json.dumps({'verified_restore_index':str(index),'scopes':selected,'cloud_files':remote}))\n*** Update File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/setup/backup/new-machine-run.py\n@@\n     if failures:\n@@\n         raise SystemExit('Backup incomplete; see '+str(log))\n+    subprocess.run([python_binary,str(here/'new-machine-publish-index.py')],stdout=out,stderr=out,check=True,creationflags=hidden)\n*** End Patch"));

chains.\\"\\"\\"\\r\\nimport hashlib, json, os, shutil, subprocess, time, uuid\\r\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\r\\nstage=Path.home()/\'LaptopBackups\'/\'2026-10-07\'\\r\\nroot_id=\'1vQ_Xm9GqDd7SlahE4sp6znQKiYkW8xOe\'\\r\\nbinary=os.environ.get(\'GWS_EXECUTABLE\') or shutil.which(\'gws\')\\r\\nif not binary: raise SystemExit(\'gws unavailable\')\\r\\nhidden=subprocess.CREATE_NO_WINDOW if os.name==\'nt\' else 0\\r\\ndef call(*args):\\r\\n    result=subprocess.run([binary,*args],capture_output=True,text=True,cwd=stage,timeout=300,creationflags=hidden)\\r\\n    if result.returncode: raise RuntimeError(\'gws index publication request failed\')\\r\\n    return json.loads(result.stdout)\\r\\nif call(\'drive\',\'about\',\'get\',\'--params\',json.dumps({\'fields\':\'user(emailAddress)\'}))[\'user\'][\'emailAddress\'].lower()!=\'contact@aspiringattorneys.com\':\\r\\n    raise SystemExit(\'Wrong Google account\')\\r\\ndef proof(directory):\\r\\n    try:\\r\\n        receipt=json.loads((directory/\'CLOUD-VERIFIED.json\').read_text())\\r\\n        if receipt.get(\'complete\') is not True or receipt.get(\'account\')!=\'contact@aspiringattorneys.com\' or receipt.get(\'generation\')!=directory.name: return None\\r\\n        if not receipt.get(\'files\') or not all(f.get(\'id\') and len(f.get(\'md5Checksum\',\'\'))==32 for f in receipt[\'files\']): return None\\r\\n        return receipt\\r\\n    except (OSError,ValueError,TypeError): return None\\r\\nselected={}\\r\\nfor path in sorted(stage.glob(\'*/manifest.json\'),reverse=True):\\r\\n    manifest=json.loads(path.read_text())\\r\\n    scope=manifest[\'scope\']\\r\\n    if scope not in [\'core\',\'personal\'] or scope in selected or not manifest[\'complete\']: continue\\r\\n    dependencies={r[\'generation\'] for r in manifest[\'files\'].values()}|{path.parent.name}\\r\\n    if not all(proof(stage/dependency) for dependency in dependencies): continue\\r\\n    receipt=proof(path.parent)\\r\\n    selected[scope]={\'generation\':path.parent.name,\'folder_id\':receipt[\'folder_id\'],\\r\\n        \'files\':len(manifest[\'files\']),\'archive_bytes\':manifest[\'archive_bytes\'],\\r\\n        \'dependencies\':sorted(dependencies)}\\r\\nif set(selected)!= {\'core\',\'personal\'}: raise SystemExit(\'Both verified backup scopes are required\')\\r\\nstamp=time.strftime(\'%Y%m%d-%H%M%S\')+\'-\'+uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]\\r\\nindex=stage/(\'Restore-Index-\'+stamp+\'.json\')\\r\\nindex.write_text(json.dumps({\'created\':time.strftime(\'%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\'),\'account\':\'contact@aspiringattorneys.com\',\'root_folder_id\':root_id,\'scopes\':selected},indent=2))\\r\\nguide=stage/(\'Restore-Guide-\'+stamp+\'.md\')\\r\\nguide.write_text(\'\'\'# Restore the verified replacement laptop backup\\r\\n\\r\\nDownload the generation folders listed below from this private folder:\\r\\nhttps://drive.google.com/drive/folders/\'\'\'+root_id+\'\'\'\\r\\n\\r\\nKeep every generation folder name unchanged inside one local folder, such as C:\\\\\\\\Backups.\\r\\nDownload every dependency listed in the index, including partial historical generation\\r\\nfolders: their verified archive files are required by the final complete manifest.\\r\\nInstall Python 3.12 or newer. Use new-machine-restore.py from the selected core folder.\\r\\n\\r\\n```powershell\\r\\npython C:\\\\\\\\Backups\\\\\\\\\'\'\'+selected[\'core\'][\'generation\']+\'\'\'\\\\\\\\new-machine-restore.py --generation C:\\\\\\\\Backups\\\\\\\\\'\'\'+selected[\'core\'][\'generation\']+\'\'\' --output C:\\\\\\\\RestoredCore\\r\\npython C:\\\\\\\\Backups\\\\\\\\\'\'\'+selected[\'core\'][\'generation\']+\'\'\'\\\\\\\\new-machine-restore.py --generation C:\\\\\\\\Backups\\\\\\\\\'\'\'+selected[\'personal\'][\'generation\']+\'\'\' --output C:\\\\\\\\RestoredPersonal\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\nBoth output folders must be new. The script verifies parts, reassembles archives and\\r\\nverifies every restored file SHA256. Map RestoredCore\'s ai-os to your new user\'s dev/ai-os;\\r\\nuse manifest roots for Codex, Claude, Superwhisper and the other folders. Apply database\\r\\nfiles only while the corresponding app is closed. Copy personal folders separately.\\r\\nReview saved user environment paths before applying them, especially Git Bash and PATH.\\r\\nInstall apps and reconnect accounts when Windows-bound credentials cannot be reused.\\r\\n\\r\\nThe index selects only generations whose whole dependency chain was cloud verified.\\r\\nUse the newest Restore-Index and matching Restore-Guide in the root folder, rather than\\r\\nselecting an in-flight generation solely by its timestamp. No files are deleted by these\\r\\nscripts. No credential or personal file is sent to GitHub.\\r\\n\'\'\',encoding=\'utf-8\')\\r\\nremote=[]\\r\\nfor path in [index,guide]:\\r\\n    digest=hashlib.md5(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest(); size=path.stat().st_size\\r\\n    params={\'q\':\\"trashed=false and \'%s\' in parents and name=\'%s\'\\"%(root_id,path.name),\'fields\':\'files(id,name,size,md5Checksum)\',\'pageSize\':100}\\r\\n    found=call(\'drive\',\'files\',\'list\',\'--params\',json.dumps(params))[\'files\']\\r\\n    matching=[f for f in found if f.get(\'md5Checksum\')==digest and int(f.get(\'size\',-1))==size]\\r\\n    result=matching[0] if matching else call(\'drive\',\'files\',\'create\',\'--json\',json.dumps({\'name\':path.name,\'parents\':[root_id]}),\'--upload\',str(path),\'--params\',json.dumps({\'fields\':\'id,name,size,md5Checksum\'}))\\r\\n    verified=call(\'drive\',\'files\',\'get\',\'--params\',json.dumps({\'fileId\':result[\'id\'],\'fields\':\'id,name,size,md5Checksum\'}))\\r\\n    if verified.get(\'md5Checksum\')!=digest or int(verified.get(\'size\',-1))!=size: raise SystemExit(\'Index upload checksum mismatch\')\\r\\n    remote.append(verified)\\r\\nprint(json.dumps({\'verified_restore_index\':str(index),\'scopes\':selected,\'cloud_files\':remote}))\\r\\n"}'}]
